## 2026-11-09 M1, G6's second fix: item 3 lists by read set, P45 (issue #108)

G6's builder, the last fix turn, after G5's last fix (2daab6e), on the review of G6's fix (`tmp/m1/review-g6-fix.md`: blocking 5, one cause) and pick P45. A second addendum to G6's entry above. Every probe of G6's review (`tmp/m1/g6-review/`), of the fix's review (`tmp/m1/g6-fix-review/`) and G6's own (`tmp/m1/g6/`) was run ask by ask on refcore and the engine (`tmp/m1/g6/fix/cmpset.py`, the sets in `sets.txt`): before on the release binary of f8635f5's item 3 (`before2.txt`, `before2.diffs`), after on this commit's debug binary (`after2.txt`, `after2.diffs`).

- **The rule as built (P45; `invariant.rs` `scenario_unchanged`, `instance_facts`, `listed`, `answers_itself`).** A plan instance before `from` is listed when its read set (E §3.2) holds a fact of the scenario the world under it doesn't see. The read set is a new `Collected.scenario`, (section, item) pairs, merged wherever `Collected` merges: through defs and memo cells, and through the balances an instance reads, since a balance's register (`cum`) carries the movements' own. Noted at the reads: a row of a scenario's version (`eval/read.rs` `row_converted`, `eval/rules.rs`), a thing's entries, place and key things a scenario states, at a membership test on a day and a binding over things (`Engine::note_thing`, `Reality::thing_scenario_sections`), and a scenario's move a binding over an event kind ranges over (`book/members.rs` `movements_of`). A reading or pad that differs at a place a listed instance changed on that day or earlier follows from it (replaces NB2's "every later day"). A hole, a hole no reading answers and a refusal pass and aren't listed, as they answer the read (findings 3, 4). Anything else is `ENGINE-INVARIANT … differs from the base's, and nothing it read is the scenario's`. The line prints the items read, not the versions (NB6): `before from: pay on 2026-03-01 reads fee`; a removed instance `before from: pay on 2026-03-01 removed by less`.

## 2026-11-09 M1, G6's third fix: a decision before `from` no longer switches item 3 off, P46 (issue #108)

G6's builder, a short turn after T's C6 and C7 (3e3cd91, f8467e5), on T's find (law scenario-before-19, vector `fixes/g6/decided_b1`) and pick P46. A third addendum to G6's entry above.

- **Cause:** `scenario_unchanged` still passed the whole walk when any decision ran before `from` (refcore's `m_inv3` skip, kept in both fixes). `decided`'s `sweep` (`reads konto`) is a decision, so `pay`, booked before `from` with the scenario's fee read at a law date after it, was printed with no `before from:` line: a silent wrong result.
- **Fix (`invariant.rs` `scenario_unchanged`, `instance_facts`, `add_read`, `listed`):** the skip is gone. A decision's instance as the walk ran it (`walk.runs`) joins the plan instances' read sets, so a decision is checked like any movement and listed by what it read, through the balances it read too. A decision that differs and whose read set holds nothing of the scenario stops `NotBuilt` ("its projection's reads untraced"), never `ENGINE-INVARIANT`: a decision's projection doesn't merge its rows into the decision's read set yet (P45's untraced kind).
- **Other movements before `from` that read a balance:** `tmp/m1/g6/fix/dec/after.tally` (the sweep moved after `pay`, so it reads the balance `pay` changed) and `viadef.tally` (the same, through `def excess = max(0, bal(konto) - floor)`) both list `pay … reads fee` and `sweep on 2026-04-01 reads fee`; before, both printed `50.00 PLN` with no line. A plan move doesn't read a balance (only a decision's `reads` does), so the decision covers the transfer that reads a balance.
- **Vector and law:** `decided_b1` now prints `before from: pay on 2026-03-01 reads fee` (it differs from refcore, as its held row for #121 expects); `m1_properties`' scenario-before-19 is green; `milestone-report: M1: 51/68 green` (50 before).
- **Probes, before → after (the 110 asks of the last fix, plus `dec/`'s and the vector's 6):** before 83 byte-equal, 33 differ; after 80 byte-equal, 36 differ; none past 60 s. The three new differences are the three `b1`s above (the ruled answer, #121); the other 33 are the last fix's, unchanged.

## 2026-11-09 M1, G7: axes at run: the options in the context, an ask's lines fanned out per combination, a timeline per combination the moves reach (issue #108)

The builder of the M1 run, group G7 of `tmp/m1/build-plan.md`, on ecddca7. Each red id was reproduced first on the debug binary: all four stopped `tally run: not implemented` at `ask/answer.rs`'s "an axis at run". Edges were probed on refcore and the engine with G4's `tmp/m1/g4/both.py` (probes in `tmp/m1/g7/`; the four checks against their stored outputs by `tmp/m1/g7/cmp.py`).

- **The axis options in the context (L §5.5; E §3.2, §3.3).** `eval/context.rs`: `LayerData.axes`, each axis's option by its id, interned with the pins, so one combination is one layer (`Engine::with_options`). A def's cell keeps only the options of the axes it reaches (`def_axes`: load's `reach_axes` from the def, cached), and where it reads the timeline those the walk reaches too (`walk_axes`: what the cone of moves, places, prices, parts and fills reads by id, as a key or as a mode); `walk_pins` keeps the walk's, so there is one timeline per combination of the axes the moves reach (G6's `TimelineKey.pins`), at most 64 by `LOAD-FORKS`. An axis read by id is its option (`axis_option`: `Value::Key { index: axis, name: option }`); read outside the ask's combinations (its `law`, `as known`, a `with` pin's key) it stops `NotBuilt`, where refcore stops at its interpreter guard. `with a = opt` and `as if {a = opt}` set it, listed `pinned: a = opt` (refcore's `apply_pins_`, its axis arm); a `with`-pinned axis doesn't fan out (load's `ask_axes`).
- **The option at its uses.** `eval/expr.rs` `name` (the axis by id) and `resolve_word` (an option literal beside it); `eval/read.rs` `literal_key`, `to_key` over `Domain::Axis` (an input or def keyed by an axis, read at `x[ax]`), `option_at`; `eval/arith.rs` `key_order` (options in the order written: `ra < r2`); `eval/builtin.rs` `rounding_mode` (an axis as `round`'s and `prorata`'s mode).
- **The fan-out (L §5.5, §5.18; refcore's `_answer_in`, `binds_over_axes`, `group`, `split`).** New `ask/axes.rs`: the combinations, the first axis slowest. With a `for`, every combination's tuples first: a tuple first bound in a later combination stands after the one bound before it there, and a tuple whose `where` is a hole in any combination prints as that hole, its questions joined, unevaluated. Then each combination's layer is open while its tuples' lines are read. Two results are equal when their lines print the same text; each set of combinations of one result is split into products by the axes in id order (a loop over a stack, R-16), and a tuple's lines are sorted by each axis's first option, `*` first. Head `q (v = x) [a = *, b = x | y]: …`; register `reading: b = x | y` after `world:` and before `before from:` (`ask/register.rs`; no vector prints the two together). A `check` and a `solve` fan out alike. `windows each` over axes (refcore's `each_over_axes`, `boxes`) isn't built: `windows each` itself stops `NotBuilt`.
- **The year and the period literal beside a key of a period index (L §2.1, §2.5; refcore's `resolve_key_literal` in `compare`).** UL-02's `cy_of[m] == 2026`, `cy` a `year 2025..2026 from 02-01`, was false: the engine read 2026 as the calendar year, refcore as the key 2026 labels (2026-02-01 to 2027-01-31). Probing its neighbours found a month literal beside a key of a `month … by 3` index false the same way (`by3.tally` `eq_month`). `eval/years.rs` `period_like` takes the key of that label on the other side's grid, for a year form and for a written month or quarter (2631281). G1's area, met here.
- **Green ids (4 of 4):** `U:num/NUM-04`, `I:M:UL-01`, `I:M:UL-02`, `I:M:UL-05` (`m1_checks_g7`), each byte-equal with its stored refcore output: the three models, M1's first `I:M:` cases, answer as refcore does, every line and register. `milestone-report: M1: 51/68 green` before, `M1: 52/68 green` after.
- **Probes (`tmp/m1/g7/`), 43 runs, 39 byte-equal:** `ax1` (13: one axis, two, a set that isn't a product, `with` and `as if` axis pins, a `where` over an axis and its tuple order, `check`, a tuple of three, a keyed input, the mode, the options' order), `ax2` (6: a move's amount reading an axis, so a timeline per option; `bal`, `flows`, a hole under one axis, `solve`), `ax3` (5: an input keyed by an axis in two versions, a `where` hole, `prorata` by an axis mode, value pins keyed by an axis), `ax4` (2: six axes, 64 combinations, every one read by a move), `ax5` (3: a def keyed by an axis), `sc/` (6: asks in a scenario's and the model's world, `as if {scenario}`, `diff`), `cy`, `by3`, `by3c` (9: the year and period literals). The four that differ: `ax2 q_law` (an axis in the ask's `law`) and `ax3 q_pinkey` (a `with` pin keyed by an axis): refcore `REFCORE-NOT-YET … read outside the axes the ask reaches`, the engine `not implemented`, both unbuilt; `sc w_diff`, the STOP-REPORT below; `by3c a_paren` (`first_q == (2026-04)`): the engine `true`, as refcore answers the bare twin, refcore `false` (P23, #113: a bracketed literal is the literal; for T to hold).
- **Seen, not built:** a binding's movements' registers under axes are kept with each combination's line (`Member.brought`), as the engine's line without axes keeps them; refcore's `binds_over_axes` drops them (unprobed: no bound movement with an unfulfilled instance under an axis). The order the lines are read in differs from refcore's (combination by combination, so each combination's cells are shared by its tuples, against refcore's tuple by tuple); the same lines are read, so only which of two load refusals at run comes first could differ (none probed).
- **Files** (paths under `crates/tally/src/`), for the map (P19): new `ask/axes.rs`: an ask's fan-out over its axes, the tuples across combinations, the lines grouped by result and split into products. Changed: `ask/{answer, register}.rs`, `eval/{arith, builtin, context, expr, read, years}.rs`, `lib.rs`, `load/pins.rs` (`reach_axes` opened to the crate). No file split.
- **Size:** 11 files, +917 −92 in engine/crates from ecddca7 (planned +330 −50, expected +530 −80): about 1.7 times the expected size. `axes.rs` is 563 lines (95 of doc, 45 of its own tests); `context.rs` +186 net (the axes through the layer, the trim and the walk's axes, the axis pin). What drove it: the plan sized the fan-out from refcore's `group` and `split` (about 60 lines); `binds_over_axes`'s tuple order and its holes, the products' heads, readings and sort, and the axis at each use (key, mode, order, literal) are about as much again, and the period literal fix is +40.
- **Kernel functions touched:** none (`num/`, `read/`, `walk/`, `book/`, `hole/` unchanged), so no mutation batch.
- **Exceptions:** +0 (6); report-only hits 8, as locked. The code had two more, nested closures (`eval/arith.rs` `key_order`, `eval/read.rs` `axis_has`), restructured through `option_at`.
- **Check:** `check full: green, 31 steps (670s)` on 2631281; `milestone-report: M1: 52/68 green`. The first `check full`, on 2db1be4, was "check full: green, 31 steps (1551s)".
- **Totals:** three commits: 2db1be4 (the axes), 2631281 (the period literal), and this entry. Cost about 0.96 % of the week at this entry (`engine/tools/usage`), inside the brief's 0.8–1.3 %.
- **Next:** G8 inherits `LayerData.axes` and `Engine::with_options`, `axis_option`, `def_axes`, `walk_axes` (`eval/context.rs`), and `ask/axes.rs`'s `fanned_lines`, through which every ask that reaches an axis is answered: a projection's or a balance's read under axes walks the combination's timeline, as `ax2`'s `bal` and `flows` do. G11: `explain` and `inputs-of` fan out in refcore (its `print_line` has their arms), `readers-of` doesn't (`_answer_in` returns it before the axes); `form_line` is where they join. ## STOP-REPORT 2026-11-09: a `diff` ask whose expression reads an axis: refcore prints every key unchanged (its guard refuses both worlds' reads alike); the spec doesn't say how a diff fans out

- **Kind:** the spec seems silent (refcore answers wrongly; the engine stops not built)
- **Where:** refcore `ask.py` `_answer_in` (a `diff` returns through `answer_diff` before the axes fan out, so its reads meet `evaluate.py` `axis_option`'s guard, `REFCORE-NOT-YET axis … read outside the axes the ask reaches`, and `diff_combine` finds the two worlds' refusals equal); the engine's `ask/answer.rs` `ask_body` (a diff never fans out, and its axis read stops `NotBuilt`, exit 69).
- **Rule or gate:** L §5.5 Fan-out ("an ask evaluates every combination of the axes its evaluation reads … one line per distinct result"), §5.18 (`diff(e) in S against B`: per line `added`, `removed` or `changed`, the unchanged counted); DONE gate 10.
- **What happens:** `tmp/m1/g7/sc/model.tally` with `sc/cheap.tally` (a scenario that halves `rate[q1]`; `fee[k]` rounds by the axis `rm` and doubles under the axis `share`), `python3 tmp/m1/g4/both.py tmp/m1/g7/sc w_diff model.tally cheap.tally`: refcore `w_diff: diff in cheap against axw: 0 changed, 0 added, 0 removed, 2 unchanged` (exit 0), though `fee[q1]` is 10.01 PLN against 5.00 PLN in every combination; the engine `tally run: not implemented` (exit 69). With both axes pinned (`w_pin_diff`, `with rm = down, share = one`) both print `1 changed`, `changed total: 25.01 PLN against 30.01 PLN`, byte-equal.
- **What I tried:** nothing on refcore or the tests; no vector reaches it. The engine doesn't guess a layout: a diff's lines are keyed by its binding tuple, and how they meet L §5.5's `[a = …]` heads and `reading:` entries is unwritten.
- **What I ask:** a ruling, for the spec at M2's round. (Recommended) A diff fans out like any ask: each combination's diff of the two worlds is the result, combinations giving the same diff lines print together under one head `q [a = …]: diff in S against B: …` with `reading:` in its register, split into products as L §5.5 says; refcore fixed at the round, the engine building it then and stopping not built until. Or: a diff that reaches an axis its pins don't fix is refused at load. T to hold `w_diff` for the ruling. Whether refcore's wrong count is the brief's patch case (a valid text, though not a number) is the coordinator's call.
- **State:** 2631281 carries G7's code; nothing of this is built. No test, spec or refcore file touched.

## 2026-11-09 M1, G8: the written projections and the walk's reads: `low`, `gap`, `value` with prices, a pad's flow (issue #108)

The builder of the M1 run, group G8 of `tmp/m1/build-plan.md`, on 5aa0f08. Each red id was reproduced first on the debug binary: the four checks stopped `tally run: not implemented` at `low`, `value` and `gap`, HOLE-PRICE's fixture at `value`, the two `low` laws at `low`. Edges were probed on refcore and the engine with G4's `tmp/m1/g4/both.py` (probes in `tmp/m1/g8/`).

- **`low(x, a .. b) [from t]` (L §5.14.1, E §4.3; refcore's `m_e_low`).** `walk/projection.rs` `low`: with `from t`, the projection's own walk (`proj_walk`, the one `bal … from t` reads) walked to b and read at the end of each day a..b (`projected_days`), with its clauses (`with uncertain`, the read's or the cell's context's) and its holder's dependents left out and listed, as a projection's; without `from`, the plain balances (`plain_days`, through `bal_day`). `lowest`: the first of equal lows; a hole on any day spreads, with every question. A day before t: `RUN-DOMAIN low(x, a .. b) from t: a day before the projection's start`; t before S: the run's-start hole (`HOLE-PRERUN`).
- **`gap(x, d)` (L §5.19, E §6.1; refcore's `m_c_gap`).** `book/reading.rs` `apply_stated` keeps each applied `stated` reading's gap with what it rests on (`GapAt`, in `Walk.gaps` by place and day); `walk/reads.rs` `gap` reads the latest instant's last one in the main walk at the cut, its register absorbed; none that day: `HOLE-NOREADING stated[x] has no reading at d`.
- **A pad's flow at its reading's place (L §5.19; refcore's `flows_place_walk`, `apply_reading`).** The reset a pad books at the reading's place carries the pad's flow (`Ent.flow`), so `flows` of the padded place counts it (`walk/flows.rs` `place_log`, `count`), a hole gap as its hole.
- **`value(x, d) in CUR [by p]` (L §5.10, §3.4, §4.6, §8.1; refcore's `m_e_value`, `m_priced_read`, `m_price_read`).** `walk/reads.rs` `valuation`: the positions at the end of d (a place of unit `any`: the units its log holds); a position in CUR at its amount, a zero one skipped; a place priced whole (`priced p`, p money) at p, in CUR through its `by` when p is in another currency; a position in unit u by the place's price clause keyed by the place or by a kind holding u, by an expression of type `num CUR/u` (`RUN-DOMAIN` in another unit, `RUN-NONE` where it is none), by `last(px)` unkeyed, or by a price input or def keyed by unit; `HOLE-PRICE <u> has no price in <CUR> on <d>` naming `pid[keys]`; `LOAD-PRICE`, `LOAD-UNIT`, `LOAD-KEY` where load lets a text by that the run meets. `eval/read.rs` `price_at`: a price at its keys on the value's day, through its carry with `last`; with no reading on or before d it is `none` (so `HOLE-PRICE`, as refcore), not `HOLE-NOREADING` (`Past::Price`). `model/reality.rs` takes load's `Priced`, `price_mode` and `clause_mode`, now shared by load and the walk.
- **A per-unit price of a unit kind takes its key's unit (L §3.2; refcore's `row_value`, its end).** `input fxs[u in currencies]: num PLN/u` read at EUR printed `4.25 PLN/$u`, in a plain read, a `last` and a `held:` line (the value's figures were right: `value` multiplies the quantities): `eval/read.rs` `row_converted` now takes the read's keys and `at_keys` replaces the type's `$u`, so `4.25 PLN/EUR`, as refcore. Met by the `val3/` probe; no vector had one. G3's area, met here (57d25e0).
- **A balance read of a place of unit `any` without its unit** refuses `LOAD-UNIT bal(x, d) of a place of unit any states its unit: bal(x, u, d)` (`own_unit`, shared by `bal` and `low`).

## STOP-REPORT 2026-11-09: `value(x, d) in CUR by last(px)` and `priced last(p)`: refcore drops the `held:` line its own `last(px, d)` prints

- **Kind:** the spec seems clear and refcore differs (the engine follows the spec)
- **Where:** refcore `mreads.py` `m_last_of` (the carry `m_price_read` and `m_priced_read` read for `by last(p)` and `priced last(p)`, L §5.10) returns the row's value and notes no `held`, where `evaluate.py` `e_lastx` (`last(x[k], d)`) does; the engine's `eval/read.rs` `price_at` reads the price through `latest_at` and `last_value`, the same path as `last_read`, so both mark it.
- **Rule or gate:** L §5.10 ("a price that is a reading is read through its carry, `by last(px)` (§4.6)"), §4.6 ("Past the last row … the register marks the value `held` (§8.2), so the carry is written where it is read"), §8.2's `held:` row ("a row that `last(x[k], d)` reads past the end of its span"); DONE gate 10.
- **What happens:** `tmp/m1/g8/val/v1.tally` (`px: num PLN/etf reading`, one reading at 2026-04-01; `hut: assets PLN priced last(hut_val)`, one reading at 2026-02-01), `python3 tmp/m1/g4/both.py tmp/m1/g8/val - v1.tally`: `a_box_last` (`value(box, 2026-05-31) in PLN by last(px)`) and `a_hut` (`value(hut, 2026-04-30) in PLN`) are 604 PLN and 250 000 PLN in both; the engine adds `held: px = 151 PLN/etf (readings_2026), read for 2026-05-31` and `held: hut_val = 250 000.00 PLN (readings_2026), read for 2026-04-30`, refcore prints neither. `last(px, 2026-05-31)` alone prints the same `held:` line in both (`v2`'s `l_px`). The same with a price keyed by unit: `tmp/m1/g8/val3/v3.tally` `k_last` and `k_safe` (`by last(fxu)`), where `k_lx` (`last(fxu[EUR], 2026-03-31)`) prints `held:` in both.
- **What I tried:** nothing on refcore or the tests; no vector reaches it (all of G8's ids are green).
- **What I ask:** a ruling, for the spec's next round. (Recommended) `by last(p)` and `priced last(p)` are `last(p, d)` (L §5.10 names §4.6's carry), so they mark `held` as it does: refcore's `m_last_of` fixed at the round, T to hold `a_box_last` and `a_hut` "for #n". Or: a value's price is read in silence, and §5.10 says so.
- **State:** d8daa9a carries G8's code, built as above. No test, spec or refcore file touched.

## STOP-REPORT 2026-11-09: `last(x, d)` over a row that governs `always`: refcore finds no row, the engine reads it; the spec doesn't say whether an `always` span "began on or before d"

- **Kind:** the spec seems unclear (G3's `last`, met through `value … by last(p)`)
- **Where:** refcore `mreads.py` `m_last_of` and `evaluate.py` `e_lastx` (`candidates(…, last_before=d)` leaves the `always` row out); the engine's `read/rows.rs` `in_time` (`Test::Last`) and `eval/read.rs` `latest_row`, which take it, since `always` contains every day.
- **Rule or gate:** L §4.6 step 1 ("`always` contains every day"), its "Past the last row" (`last(x[k], d)` on a state input "reads the latest row whose span began on or before d"), §8.2's `held:` row ("A row that governs `always` is timeless and is never `held`"); DONE gate 10.
- **What happens:** `tmp/m1/g8/val/v2.tally` (`input eurpx: num PLN/EUR`, `eurpx = 4.30 PLN/EUR` in a version that governs `always`), `python3 tmp/m1/g4/both.py tmp/m1/g8/val - v2.tally`: `l_eur` (`last(eurpx, 2026-03-31)`): refcore `?"eurpx: no value at 2026-03-31" (eurpx)`, the engine `4.3 PLN/EUR`; `a_flat` (`value(flat, 2026-03-31) in PLN by last(eurpx)`, flat priced 100 000.00 EUR): refcore `?"EUR has no price in PLN on 2026-03-31" (eurpx[])`, the engine `430 000 PLN`. Same in `v1`.
- **What I tried:** nothing on refcore, the tests or G3's `last`; no vector reaches it.
- **What I ask:** a ruling. (Recommended) An `always` row is a candidate of `last(x, d)` at every d, read as itself and never `held` (§4.6 step 1, §8.2): the engine as it is, refcore's `last_before` fixed at the round, T to hold `l_eur` and `a_flat`. Or: `last` reads only rows with a start, and an `always` row under it is a hole, said in §4.6.
- **State:** d8daa9a; the engine's `last` is G3's, unchanged by G8. No test, spec or refcore file touched.

## STOP-REPORT 2026-11-09: `low(x, a .. b) with uncertain` without `from`: refcore and the engine leave the clause out, though `bal(x, d) with uncertain` counts it

- **Kind:** the spec seems unclear (the engine follows refcore)
- **Where:** refcore `mreads.py` `m_e_low` (without `from`, the plain `bal_day` per day; the read's `with uncertain` unread); the engine's `walk/projection.rs` `low`, `plain_days` (the same).
- **Rule or gate:** L §5.14.1's table: `low(x, a .. b) [from t] [clauses]` is "`min(bal(x, d) from t for d in a .. b step 1 day)` with the same clauses; without `from`, of the plain balances"; L §2 rule 7 (a clause after `low(…)` belongs to it); §5.14 (`with uncertain` on a plain `bal`).
- **What happens:** `tmp/m1/g8/lowu/l1.tally` (`gift`, an uncertain move of 400.00 PLN on 2026-03-20), `python3 tmp/m1/g4/both.py tmp/m1/g8/lowu p_u1 l1.tally` and `p_u2`: `bal(konto, 2026-04-30) with uncertain` is 3 400.00 PLN (with `uncertain: gift`), but `low(konto, 2026-04-30 .. 2026-04-30) with uncertain` is 3 000.00 PLN with no `uncertain:` line, in both.
- **What I tried:** nothing on refcore or the tests; no vector reaches it. "of the plain balances" can be read as `bal(x, d)` with the same clauses (then 3 400.00) or as the balances with no clause (then 3 000.00, as built).
- **What I ask:** a ruling. (Recommended) "With the same clauses" holds with and without `from`: `low(x, a .. b) with uncertain` is `min(bal(x, d) with uncertain …)`, refcore's `m_e_low` fixed at the round, T to hold `p_u2`, the engine following then. Or: without `from` a clause is refused at load (`LOAD-PARSE`), since a plain balance has no projection to take it.
- **State:** d8daa9a, built as refcore reads it. No test, spec or refcore file touched.

## 2026-11-09 M1, G7's fix: a move's flows beside another move's axis, a diff over its axes not built, P47 and P48 (issue #108)

G7's builder, a short turn after G8 (d8daa9a, 57d25e0, 0ccb0b7), on G7's review (`tmp/m1/review-g7.md`, its probes in `tmp/m1/g7-review/`) and picks P47 and P48. An addendum to G7's entry above.

- **B1, cause:** `flows(charge, d)` of a plan move reads no axis, so its ask doesn't fan out (load's `ask_axes`: `charge`'s reach), but counting a leg asks whether the walk refused it (`walk/flows.rs` `leg_refused`, E §4.2 step 5), and the main walk in the ask's layer reads `salary`'s amount, whose axis `pa` that layer sets no option of: `axis_option`'s `NotBuilt`. Refcore builds the same walk, meets its guard and reads no refusal (`leg_refused` swallows any error of the walk).
- **B1, fix (P48: no head, as refcore):** where the walk reaches axes the timeline in force sets no option of, `leg_refused` reads the leg's refusal in each of their combinations' main walks (`eval/timelines.rs` `unset_combinations`, `refused_in_each`; `eval/context.rs` `unset_walk_axes`). They agree, so the line reads no option and has no head; where they differ the line would be one per combination, which no ask's fan-out gives here, so it stops `NotBuilt` (unprobed: no text found where the walk refuses a leg under one option only); past 64 combinations it stops `NotBuilt` too. A combination's walk that refuses refuses none of its legs to this read, as at the ask's own timeline. These walks are a check, not the run's: their warnings are dropped (`tl`'s `WARN-NEGATIVE` otherwise, refcore walking none), and their layers and timelines are freed when each closes, so a read that walks a combination's timeline itself warns as before.
- **Other reads over a timeline another move's axis splits:** `bal`, `low`, `value`, `gap`, a place's `flows` and a decision's `flows` reach the balances, so every move: their asks fan out over `pa` (`[pa = *]`), byte-equal (G8's `ax`, the review's `fl2` and `tl`, `tmp/m1/g7/fix/fl5` `q_sweep`, `q_kasa`, `fl6` `q_gap`). Only a plan move's `flows` read the walk outside the fan-out (`leg_refused`); `fix/fl5`: a def reading it, a year span, a leg refused in every combination (`RUN-LIFESPAN`, refcore's plan refusal too), two moves in a tuple, `with pa = hi`, all byte-equal.
- **B2 (P47):** `ask/answer.rs` `ask_body`: a `diff` or `residuals` ask with a non-empty `ask_axes` stops `NotBuilt` ("a diff or residuals ask over the axes it reaches"), as at ecddca7, until #122's layout is written; `w_flat` and `w_diff` both stop now.
- **Probes, before → after (`tmp/m1/g7/allprobes.py`: every ask of G7's and the review's probes, one by one; `probes-before.tsv`, `probes-after.tsv`):** 91 asks; before 83 byte-equal, 8 differ; after 85 byte-equal, 6 differ. Turned byte-equal: the review's `fl q_fc`, `fl2 q_flows_gift`, `tl q_flows_charge`. Turned different, as ruled: `g7-review/sc w_flat` (P47: refcore answers with no head, the engine stops not built). Unchanged: `ax2 q_law`, `ax3 q_pinkey` (both unbuilt, refcore at its guard), `by3c a_paren` (#113), `w_diff` in both `sc/` (P47). New in `tmp/m1/g7/fix/`: `fl3` (3), `fl5` (7), `fl6` (3), all byte-equal.
- **Not built, from the review's NB5:** a `with x = f` pin whose value reads an axis (`eval/context.rs` `value_pin`) and an `as known` that reads an axis (`ask/answer.rs`) stop `NotBuilt` at `axis_option`, as refcore stops at its guard; they join G7's list (`law`, a `with` pin's key, `windows each`, a diff or residuals over its axes).
- **The map (NB6, R-127):** `eval/context.rs` is 1 088 lines (936 before G7, 1 070 after it, +18 here for `unset_walk_axes`); its split (the axes and the trims beside the pins' layers) is for the map's round, the fix touching that region only by the one function.
- **Size:** 4 files, +129 −6 in engine/crates (`eval/timelines.rs` +96, `eval/context.rs` +18, `walk/flows.rs` +16 −6, `ask/answer.rs` +5).
- **Kernel functions touched:** `walk/flows.rs` `leg_refused` (5 lines: the combinations' call) and `chain_refusal` (made `pub(crate)`). No mutation batch this turn: the kernel change is the one call into the eval side; for T, the review's `fl`, `fl2` and `tl` as vectors.
- **Exceptions:** +0 (6); report-only hits 8, as locked.
- **Check:** check full on the code of this commit: "check full: green, 31 steps (708s)"; `milestone-report: M1: 57/68 green` (G7's four still green; 52 at G7's entry, G8's since).
- **Cost:** this session about 1.17 % of the week at the code (0.96 % at G7's entry): about 0.21 % for this turn, inside the brief's 0.25 %.
- **Not done:** a leg's refusal that differs by an axis the ask doesn't fan out over stops `NotBuilt` (no head of the ask's can show it; for #122's round with the diff's layout); review NB1–NB4 and NB7–NB9 are T's and the map's, as the review placed them.

## 2026-11-09 M1, G8's fix: `low` over no day, a place priced whole valued by every `by`, `low`'s clauses without `from`; `value` in its own file, P51 and P53 (issue #108)

G8's builder, the fix turn, on G8's review (`tmp/m1/review-g8.md`, its probes in `tmp/m1/g8-review/`), picks P51 (#125) and P53 (#126), after G7's fix (612189a). An addendum to G8's entry above. Each blocking finding was reproduced first on the debug binary at 612189a.

- **B1 (P53):** `low(konto, 2026-03-31 .. 2026-03-01)`, with or without `from`, stopped `ENGINE-INVARIANT a low over no day` (`lowest` over an empty vector). `walk/projection.rs` `low` now refuses a window whose last day is before its first, `RUN-EMPTY low over no keys` (the catalogue's form, L §8.4, `<min, …> over no keys`, with `low` named), before its `from` and its days are read: `low` is `min(bal(x, d) … for d in a .. b step 1 day)` (L §5.14.1), whose terms an empty range never reads (L §5.11). Literal, computed (`low(konto, b_day .. a_day)`), with `from` and with `with uncertain` alike. Refcore crashes there (`REFCORE-NOT-YET … IndexError`); T holds `z_rev`, `z_revf`.
- **B2 (P53):** a place priced whole in EUR valued `in PLN by last(px)`, px a `num PLN/etf`, gave 15 100 000 PLN: `value_whole` read the price through `named_price` and took its quantity, unit unchecked. **B3 (P53):** the same place `by fx[2026-03-31]`, a written `num PLN/EUR`, was refused `LOAD-PRICE … states no by` at run, which stopped the file. Both from one cause: the whole path had its own two `by` arms. `walk/value.rs` `value_whole` now prices the place's currency as one unit of a position in it, through `position_price`, so every form of `by` takes the arm and the unit check a position's does (L §5.10: `by p` "an input or def of type `num CUR/u` … or an expression of that type written with its keys"): `z_wrong` is `RUN-DOMAIN value(flat) in PLN: the price 151 PLN/etf is not in PLN/EUR`, `z_flat_expr` 425 000 PLN, `by fx[2026-04-30]` unset its hole (`?"fx at 2026-04-30: no value for 2026-04-30"`); only a value with no `by` at all is `LOAD-PRICE`. Refcore gives 15 100 000 PLN and the `LOAD-PRICE` refusal; T holds `z_wrong`, `z_flat_expr`.
- **P51 (#125):** `low(x, a .. b)` without `from` takes the clauses a plain `bal` takes: `plain_days` reads each day `with uncertain` through `with_uncertain`, as `bal(x, d) with uncertain` does (`windows each` and `random` a plain `bal` doesn't take either). `lowu/l1` `p_u2` is `(3 400.00 PLN, 3 000.00 PLN)` with `uncertain: gift` (was `3 000.00 PLN` twice); `fix/f1` `f_unc_gift` −1 100.00 PLN, equal to its definition `min(bal(konto, d) with uncertain for d in …)` (`f_unc_gift_bal`, byte-equal with refcore). T holds `p_u2`.
- **Non-blocking, done:** `read.of` is tested before the whole-priced return (`value … of q` on a whole-priced place stops not built, no longer the whole value); `LOAD-KEY`'s text names a list or range domain ("keyed by a list of keys", "a stepped range") where it printed "keyed by , which"; `flows … for k`'s `NotBuilt` text loses "(G7)"; `value` and its price forms moved to a new file (R-127, R-120).

## 2026-11-09 M1, G7's second fix: the leg's check walks each combination once, its cap counted first, its warnings kept where the walk reads no unset axis (issue #108)

G7's builder, the last fix turn, after G8's fixes (678d88a), on the review of 612189a (`tmp/m1/review-g7-fix.md`, its probes copied to `tmp/m1/g7-fix-review/`). A second addendum to G7's entry above.

- **Blocking 1 (the cap after the product):** `unset_combinations` built every combination before `refused_in_each` tested 64: ×4 memory for each two axes. The product is now one function, `eval/timelines.rs` `combinations(counts, cap)`, shared with `ask/axes.rs` `Axes::combinations` (review NB5, R-127), which counts with a checked multiply before each axis's step and gives none past the cap, so a product past 64 is never built: past 64 the read stops `NotBuilt` at once. The combinations are only of the axes the walk reaches that the timeline sets none of, and only once the walk has read one of them (below).
- **Blocking 2 (`wn`'s lost warning):** `leg_refused` tries the main walk in the ask's timeline first, as before G7's fix; only where it stops at an axis the timeline sets no option of (`axis_option`'s stop, now the constant `UNSET_AXIS` in `eval/context.rs`) does it read each combination's walk (`refused_in_each`). So an axis reached on paper and never read leaves the line's own walk, its warnings kept (`wn`: `WARN-NEGATIVE`, as refcore). Where the combinations are walked, a warning every one of them gives is the run's, and one only some give is dropped: refcore's leg check walks to the leg's day (`guarded_walk`, `prefix_walk`) and meets there what holds before any axis is read, and nothing past its guard. That one rule fits both: the review's `c64`/`c64d` (`konto is below zero from 2026-01-01` in all 64, printed by refcore) and `tl` (negative only when paid in July, refcore prints none). `c64`'s two flows asks printed no warning at 612189a too: they weren't byte-compared then, only timed.
- **Review NB1 (no reuse):** each combination's main walk is walked once for the ask's timeline and cut, its refused legs kept with that timeline (`Timeline.each_refused`, `Engine.each_refused`, parked and freed with it); every other leg's read takes them from there (`refused_by_each`, before the retry of the unfinished walk). The cost is the combinations × a walk, not the legs × the combinations.
- **Review NB4 (B2's check after the pins):** the P47 check in `ask_body` runs after the ask's `law`, `with` pins and `before_clock`, where ecddca7 checked before them, so a pin's hole or `REQ-CLOCK` answers first; kept: both are the ask's own refusals, met before its body as for any form.
- **Probes, before (612189a, the review's figures) → after:**
  - `ax16`, `ax18`, `ax20`, `ax22` (debug, `/usr/bin/time -l`): before 0.24 GB / 0.5 s, 0.96 GB / 1.8 s, 4.37 GB / 14.5 s, not run (about 17 GB); after each stops `not implemented` at once, peak footprint about 1.9 MB, 0.00–0.7 s.
  - `wn`: before the warning lost; after byte-equal with refcore, `warning: WARN-NEGATIVE konto is below zero from 2026-03-01` printed.
  - `c64` / `c64d` (release, 64 combinations, 6 axes read by `salary`): daily `flows(charge, 2026)` 86.8 s before, 0.23 s after (refcore 0.11 s); daily `flows(charge, 2026-03-01)` 0.23 s; monthly 0.01–0.38 s; `bal(konto, end)` over all 64, 0.95 s (0.93 s before); peak 2.3–22.4 MB. All six asks byte-equal with refcore after; before, the four flows asks lacked refcore's warning.
  - `pr1`, `pr2`: `not implemented`, before and after (a leg refused under some options, or under each with other messages; refcore's `10.00 DUK` is its swallowed guard, review NB3 for #122's round).
  - `fl`, `fl2`, `tl` and every ask of G7's and the first review's probes (`allprobes.py`, 91): 85 byte-equal, 6 differ, the same as at 612189a (`q_law`, `q_pinkey`, `a_paren`, `w_diff` twice, `w_flat`); `fix/fl3`, `fl5`, `fl6` byte-equal.

## 2026-11-09 M1, G9: fence F5, a written projection's common case read from a tree of running sums; mutation batch MB3 (issue #108)

The builder of the M1 run, group G9 of `tmp/m1/build-plan.md` (§3 G9, §4 MB3), on 086988f (G7's second fix), picks P25, P26, P51 and P53 read. F5 is the first fence of M1 (R-130): its rows are P8-5 and P8-M4, its spec E §4.3's common case and P §2.3.

- **What F5 does (`walk/lows.rs`, new).** `bal(x, d) from t` and `low(x, a .. b) from t` are E §4.3's projection: x's balance at t, then x's plan legs as known at the cut c = min(t, K), with the holder's own moves left out (L). In the common case that is a start balance plus F(u), the running sum of x's counted legs through day u, and `low` is a range minimum over F (P §2.3). F5 keeps F in a segment tree, one per (place, unit, left-out set) in each timeline, built from the plan pass's instances at c the first time a read needs it; a node holds its days' sum and least running sum, so a read is a prefix sum and one range minimum, O(log n). The register the walk prints is kept beside it: each leg's register by position, x's `left out:` entries, the left-out moves' days for their `left out:` lines, the chain's register at the read's point (`chain_register`, split from `read_state`). The tree lives with its timeline (`eval/timelines.rs` parks and unparks it) and is never shared across worlds or cuts.
- **The common case, and the twin otherwise.** A read goes to the tree only when nothing it counts needs the walk. Never from a tree where x's kind has a sign rule (`min 0`, `max 0`), a counted decision or a refused instance has no day, a hole instance touching x has none, or the legs at x carry more than 64 distinct registers. Not where a day from the read's first counted day through its last holds an act the sums don't (a mark of a modelled instance nothing fulfils; a leg at x with a hole or refused amount; a leg dated off its movement's day; a leg whose other end the walk may not book: a hole, outside its lifespan, its end in question, a sign rule); nor where a hole or refused instance reaching x, or a leg's hole day, falls on t or later; nor where a counted decision runs, or x's lifespan ends, from t through the last day. Not with `with uncertain`, nor where the start balance isn't known; a read whose base walk is in another world than the timeline's is the walk's too, a guard that never fires (MB3 below). A leg of zero is skipped, as the walk skips it (`walk/day.rs` `leg`); a leg in another unit is another tree's. Every other read is the projection's own walk, G8's code unchanged (`proj_walk`, `projected_days`, `walk_projection`): the twin, `Lows::Walk`, which `run_with_lows` selects. `walk/projection.rs` offers each read to F5 first (`common_case`), after the same refusals and in the same order the twin meets them (`proj_base`, split from `proj_walk`: RUN-FUTURE, the base walk; the plan pass's first readings and the instances, as the walk reads them).
- **P51's clauses without `from` (`plain_days`):** served by the twin. A `low` without `from` reads the plain balances of the main walk (L §5.14.1), not a projection, so F5 doesn't touch it; `with uncertain` with or without `from` is on the walk.
- **Picks logged (the recommended form, none asked):** (1) a tree is rebuilt when a read's cut differs from its own, not updated in place by P §2.3's range adds as the cut rises: the plan pass at the new cut gives its instances anyway, O(I), and a rebuild is the same O(I); (2) the range add on F's suffix is a point update of the node's leg in this form (sum, least running sum), the same thing written as a monoid; (3) at most 64 distinct registers per tree, past that the twin.
- **The equivalence test (R-130):** `tally-cli/tests/walk_oracle.rs` runs every ask of every record of T's kernel corpus (312 records) and C6's `m1.corpus` (648) twice, tree and twin, and compares the lines byte for byte: all equal. The tree serves 157 of those records (171 lines): every `foresight-projection` record (72) and every `foresight-low` (72), and 13 of the 24 `low-min`; the rest read their projections through the twin (counted by a probe left uncommitted: the tree's answer moved by 1 000, the records whose lines then differ). `tests/lows/lows.tally` and `richer.tally` (new, 100 lines: an axis `pay_reading` fanning the asks out, a later version raising the rent, a sweep decision reading `low … from` through a def that leaves its own move out, a scenario `richer` over it): tree and twin equal, and two of refcore's lines pinned, one per world; the fixture's asks were byte-equal with refcore (`tmp/m1/g4/both.py`, `tmp/m1/g9/fx/`). `walk/lows.rs`'s tests: a read served by the tree with no projection walk, and by the walk with no tree; a read the sums can't hold (a hole after t) on the walk; the tree's ranges over 60 generated vectors of 0–40 legs, every range, against a fold one by one.
- **Measurement (release, niced; the Mac on battery in low-power mode from 16:47, AC before: `perf fingerprint` says so; `uptime` load 4–7 throughout):**
  - P8-M4 (`perf row P8-M4`, criterion, the run with `bal(x, E) from S` less the plain run): before (16:05, AC) 290.91 − 205.28 = 85.6 ms; after (16:47, battery) 308.91 − 299.05 = 9.9 ms, inside both medians' noise ([300.17, 317.13] and [282.87, 311.81] ms). S's first figure was 72.9 ms. Its first figure for the rules step: P8-M4 308.91 ms, P8-M4-plain 299.05 ms, on battery; to be retaken on AC at the tag.
  - The release binaries of 086988f (built at its check, 12:44) and of G9 alternated run by run, the same power state for both, 7–9 runs each, wall time of a whole `tally run` (`tmp/m1/g9/ab.py`, probes `p8m4*.tally`, `reads*.tally` there): `bal(x, E) from S` 405.8 → 298.9 ms, the plain run 316.8 / 324.8 ms; `low(x, 2030-01-01 .. 2050-12-31) from 2026-01-01` 495.8 → 294.3 ms; ten reads `low(x, d .. 2050-12-31) from d` 1 264.7 → 313.9 ms (about 95 ms a read before). Every output equal before and after.

## 2026-11-09 M1, G9's fix: a failed projection walk answers the reads before its day (P54, #127); the tree kept and updated in place (P §2.3); P8-5's profile (issue #108)

G9's builder, the fix turn, on G9's review (`tmp/m1/review-g9.md`, its probes in `tmp/m1/g9-review/p/`) and pick P54, after 8eac501. An addendum to G9's entry above. B1 was reproduced first on the debug binary at 8eac501.

- **B1, the twin (P54):** a projection's walk that fails on day D refused every later read (`walk/day.rs` `walk_through`, `Progress::Failed`), so a read whose window ends before D was refused or answered as the reads came. `Progress::Failed` now holds the day the walk was walking (`walk/state.rs`; none for a failure of the plan pass or the close, which still refuses every read), and `before_failure` (`walk/day.rs`) answers a read through a day before D from the walk as it stood there; one that reaches D, or reads to the walk's end, is refused as before. Main walks always read to their end, so only projections change.
- **B1, the tree:** it already served a read ending before a decision's failure, since a counted decision's day sends every read whose window holds it to the walk. It now also asks whether the projection's own walk (`proj_walks` by `ProjKey`) has failed on or before the read's last day (`fails_by`), so a failure of another kind, met by an earlier read through the walk (a number past the cap at another place, review NB8), refuses it there too.
- **B1, both orders, twin and tree alike:** `f_long_short: (refused: RUN-TIE pick: its ties by is a hole, 800.00 DUK)` and `f_short_long: (800.00 DUK, refused: RUN-TIE pick: its ties by is a hole)`; `f_short` 800.00 DUK, `f_long` refused, as before. Refcore gives `(refused …, refused …)` for the first until M2; T holds it for #127. The review's text is now `tests/lows/failed.tally`, pinned in `walk_oracle.rs`.
- **The tree kept across cuts (review NB1, P §2.3):** F changes only where a cut starts to see more. `knowledge_days` gathers every version's `known` and window's last day, every row's, given's and observation's own, each with the day after it, whichever of `<=` and `<` a test reads (L §4.5, §5.3, §5.19). A tree built at cut c holds for every cut from c up to the next of them (`Tree::upto`, `holds_at`). There the plan pass gives every instance a read counts (dated t or later, so after both cuts) the same day, amount and status: an amount is read at min(d, cut) as known then, `held` is judged against the ask's cut K, not the projection's (`read/rows.rs` `held`), and the day-against-cut tests (past, modelled) touch only instances before t, which no read from t counts. Past such a day the instances are gathered again. Where x's days are the same, each position whose sum changed is set in place and the nodes above it joined again (`Sums::updated`, `join`: P §2.3's range add from that day on, O(log n) each). Where the days change, or the cut goes back (a later read from an earlier t), the tree is built again.
- **Measured (release, niced, AC power, load 2.7–7; the binaries of 8eac501 and of this fix run in turn):**
  - the review's `many_cuts` (105 reads from days before K): `c_many` 18.9 → 6.2 ms, `c_sum` 10.7 → 5.5 ms;
  - G9's 33-year place read from 31 and 365 days before K (`tmp/m1/g9/cuts_big.tally`): 3 491 → 412 ms and 53 720 → 448 ms (a plan pass and a build per new cut before, about 140 ms each; one build after);
  - P8-M4 (`perf row P8-M4`, criterion): 211.28 against 204.43 ms plain (6.85 ms) before this fix, 194.31 against 192.46 ms (1.85 ms) after, inside the medians' noise; on AC, so these replace G9's battery figures for the rules step. Before G9 it was 85.6 ms, S's first figure 72.9 ms.
- **P8-5's profile** (samply at 10 kHz, as `perf profile` runs it, on the release CLI; `tmp/m1/g9/prof_low.txt`, `prof_plain.txt`): 12 053 served `low(x, d .. 2050-12-31) from d` reads in one ask, against 12 053 plain `bal(x, d)`, on the 33-year place.
  - A served read is about 3.1 µs: 2.5 µs in `low`, and 0.6 µs of the ask's comprehension around it.
  - Inside the 2.5 µs: the range minimum 0.91 µs (`lowest_change`: two queries, about 56 node joins, each two exact rational adds and a compare: `Num::checked_add` and its gcd, `u128` division); the chain's register 0.56 µs and the start state 0.31 µs, the same calls a plain read makes; the register's copies and merges about 0.4 µs; keys, the base walk and the note about 0.3 µs.
  - A plain read is about 2.3 µs: 1.66 µs in `bal` (`read_state` 1.24 µs: the state, the chain's register, its copies) and 0.64 µs of the comprehension.
  - In `walk/lows.rs`: the range minimum and the register, about 1.3 µs. Exact rationals make up most of the range minimum; summing whole minor units where every leg is one would be the next step inside the fence, not built here. The rest is the read machinery every balance read pays, outside the fence: the STOP-REPORT below.
- **Review NB5 (R-127), shared:** `agenda.rs` `one_more_left_out` (the count and first day of a move outside the run, the walk's `note_left_out` and the tree's `outside_run`), `left_out_day` (a left-out instance's day, both `count_left_out`s) and `left_out_lines` (the `left out:` lines by halving, `proj_regs` and the tree; the walk's days are now sorted once after its plan pass, as the tree's are); `day.rs` `place_sign` (the tree's `sign_of` is gone). NB6 (R-126): `judge_plan`'s left-out arm is one call. NB7: the header and `lowest_change` say "two queries of the tree"; `through`'s doc is one count.

F5's tests in it are `the_tree_prints_what_the_projections_walk_prints_on_both_corpora`, `…_under_axes_and_in_a_scenario` and `…_across_cuts_and_after_a_failure`. Beside them, the unit tests in `engine/crates/tally/src/walk/lows.rs` (package `tally`, its lib target, `walk::lows::tests::*`). mutants-local's and CI's catchers are `tally-runner`'s alone today. Not changed here.

## STOP-REPORT 2026-11-09: P8-5's 0.3 µs is a range minimum's; a read through the ask costs about 2.3 µs before F5 starts

- **Kind:** a budget looks wrong (it times something other than what its micro form measures)
- **Where:** `engine/spec/PERFORMANCE.md` §8.1 row P8-5 ("one range minimum over F (§2.3, about 14 steps) … ≤ 0.3 µs", "its micro form P8-M4") and §8.4 row P8-M4 ("≤ 0.3 µs (P8-5) … the run with the projection less the plain run"); `tools/perf-budgets.toml` P8-5 and P8-M4.
- **Rule or gate:** PERFORMANCE §8 (budgets set from a first measurement, by the rules role at M1).
- **What happens:** measured at G9's fix (AC power, release; the entry above, `tmp/m1/g9/prof_low.txt`, `prof_plain.txt`):
  - one served `low … from t` read, through an ask, is about 3.1 µs: 0.6 µs of the ask's evaluation, 1.2 µs of the balance read's own machinery that a plain `bal(x, d)` pays too (the start state, the chain's register, register copies), 0.91 µs for the range minimum itself and about 0.4 µs for F5's register;
  - a plain `bal(x, d)` read through the same ask is about 2.3 µs;
  - P8-M4's form, a whole run with the projection less the plain run, measures one read and the tree's build: 1.85 ms here, against S's 72.9 ms and 85.6 ms before F5.
- **What I think is wrong:** the row, not the code. 0.3 µs is the prototype's range minimum in machine integers. It is not what a read costs here, nor what P8-M4 measures: no design of the fence reaches it while one balance read through an ask costs 2.3 µs. The range minimum in the fence is 0.91 µs in exact rationals. Whole minor units could bring it near the prototype's figure, though not the read.
- **What I ask:** a ruling for the rules turn. (Recommended) P8-5 times the range minimum alone (a criterion bench of `Tree::lowest_change` over n = 12 054, its own budget from that figure × 1.2). P8-M4 keeps its form, a run less the plain run, its budget set from its first AC figure (1.85 ms at G9's fix) × 1.2. A read's own budget, if wanted, is a third row through the ask, with the plain `bal` beside it (about 2.3 µs). Or: P8-5 and P8-M4 stay one row, its budget set from P8-M4's figure, and the 0.3 µs is dropped.
- **What I did not do:** change a budget, a bench or a tool; optimise the read machinery outside the fence.
- **State:** this fix's commit; no test, spec or tool file touched.

## 2026-11-09 M1, G9's second fix: the tree gathered again at each new cut (B1), a booked A₀ without its instance's day (B2), the range minimum in whole minor units (P56, #128) (issue #108)

G9's builder, the last fix turn, on G9's fix review (`tmp/m1/g9-fix-review.md`, its probes in `tmp/m1/g9-fix-review/p/`) and pick P56, after d145ef2 and the rules step's commits (4cc2678, 09847a8, 85467b8, 71c463e, a1757e4), read first. A second addendum to G9's entry above. B1 and B2 were reproduced first.

- **B1, what changes with the cut alone** (every day-against-cut test in the library read, not only the knowledge days d145ef2 kept a tree between):
  - an instance's status in the plan pass: dated before the cut it is past, or modelled where a version covers its place (`walk/plan.rs` `status`; `walk/order.rs` alike in the walk);
  - an amount, day or `when` that reads one: `flows` of a past instance and its register (`unfulfilled:`), a balance (a walk at the booking cut);
  - an expression that reads the cut itself: `cut` (`eval/expr.rs`, `eval/ty.rs`), an extreme over bindings that reach past it (`eval/extremes.rs` `reaches_past`), members and `fulfils` (observations booked before the cut, `book/members.rs`);
  - on the past side only, which F doesn't hold: readings, standing observations and adjustments booked before the cut (`agenda.rs`, `order.rs`, `book/adjust.rs`), all in the start state each read takes at its own cut.
- **B1, handled:** a tree holds for reads at its own cut only. At any other cut the plan pass's instances are gathered again (the reviewer's option 3), and where x's days are the same each changed position is updated in place, else the tree is built again; going back to an earlier cut is the same. `knowledge_days`, `mark`, `Trees::next_known`, `Tree::upto` and `holds_at` are gone. Options 1 and 2 would keep a tree only where no counted amount reads a status, a balance or the cut, through any def: that needs a reads analysis of every move's body, which no budget row asks for yet. The header and `Tree::cut`'s doc say so (review NB1); NB4 goes with `knowledge_days`.
- **B1, the review's texts:** `cov_two` (100.00 DUK, 800.00 DUK) and `cov_two_rev` (800.00 DUK, 100.00 DUK) in `cov_x`; `cov_two` in `cov_nocov` with its register `unfulfilled: rent_pay[2026-03]`; refcore's, and the same under the tree and the twin.
- **B2, the cause:** the engine's A₀ compared each leg's day, and a leg with no `on` of its own takes its instance's day. That day is read at the ask's cut K, the rest at the booking cut min(t, K) (E §4.1 step 2). Refcore's `body_key` keeps a leg's own day only. The spec agrees with refcore: A₀ is what the booking cut reads (E §9 item 5). `Booked` now holds `BookedLeg`s, each with its own day where the leg states `on` (`walk/plan.rs` `booked_legs`). `inv_min`'s `z: 1 950.00 DUK`, refcore's, under both; the review's `u_day_new` matches refcore too.
- **P56, the range minimum in whole minor units:** where every position's legs are a whole number of x's unit's minor units (which holds wherever every leg is) and their sizes added fit an `i64`, the tree's nodes hold those integers (`Fig::Minor`), so no running sum can pass it. Elsewhere they stay exact rationals (`Fig::Exact`). The one result is turned back into the unit at the end (`Fig::quantity`). A tree updated into the other kind is built again (`Sums::updated`).
- **The public entry for P8-5's bench (R-68, documented):** `tally::walk::RangeMinimum::of(days, steps, minor)`, `lowest_change(counts, (first, last))` and `in_minor_units()`. It is the fence's own `Tree::lowest_change` over the same `Sums`, built from days and per-day sums as a gathered tree holds them. A bench at P8-5's size gives it 12 054 days, one step each (1.25 DUK in P8-M4's reality), with the minor unit 0.01. `tally::run_with_lows` with `tally::walk::Lows::{Tree, Walk}` is public; its doc now says that both print the same bytes and a difference is the fence's fault (P57).
- **Measured** (release, niced, AC power, load 2.4–3.2):
  - **P8-5:** the rules step's bench waits for the entry above, so it couldn't run here. Instead, samply at 10 kHz on the release CLI, 12 053 served `low(x, d .. 2057-12-31) from d` reads in one ask (`tmp/m1/g9/reads2.tally` `low_all`): `Tree::lowest_change` inclusive was 11.0 ms before (d145ef2, 0.91 µs a read). After, it is 3.3 and 4.2 ms in two runs, 0.27–0.35 µs a read (`tmp/m1/g9/prof_p56_1.txt`, `_2.txt`). That is at the 0.3 µs budget, inside the profile's sampling; the bench decides. What is left: three halvings over the days (`counts`, `first`, `last`), two queries of about 28 node joins each, now integer adds and compares, and one multiply back into the unit.
  - **P8-M4** (`perf row P8-M4`): 2.87 ms, then 1.91 ms (budget 2.22 ms), against medians about 195 ms wide in their noise.
  - **B1's cost,** a plan pass per new cut again (`tmp/m1/g9/ab.py`, medians of 3; G9's first commit / d145ef2 / now): `c_many` 18.6 / 6.2 / 18.0 ms, `c_sum` 10.4 / 5.4 / 10.4 ms, `cuts_month` (31 cuts on the 33-year place) 3 439 / 407 / 3 430 ms. The in-place update now saves only the build.
- **Re-run, before → after:**

At 8eac501: 3 DIFF (`fail_cut` twice, `f_reg`: P54). At d145ef2, the fix review's own run: `cov`, `cov_nocov`, `cov_x`. Now: 46 SAME.
  - engine against refcore, every ask of the same sets (`rc-after.tsv`): 223 asks, 216 SAME. The 7 DIFF are P54's rulings (`f_long_short` in both copies of fail_cut, `g_a`, `g_b`, `f_dec_short`'s `d_proj`; refcore at M2) and NB5's two decisions on one day reading one projection (`f_dec`'s `d_main` and `d_proj`: not built, in both engines). Before, `cov_x`, `cov_nocov` (`cov_two`, `cov_low`), `u_day_new` and `inv_min` (B2) differed too.
  - the equivalence test, `tally-cli::walk_oracle` `…_across_cuts_and_after_a_failure`, now holds `cov_x`, `cov_nocov` and `inv_min` (new fixtures in `tests/lows/`), with refcore's lines pinned and `cov_two`'s register checked; green, and both corpora too.
  - the two `low` laws, `m1_low_is_the_minimum_of_the_daily_balances` and `m1_no_foresight_through_low`: PASS.
  - the unit tests: `a_tree_is_gathered_again_at_each_new_cut` (it was `a_tree_holds_until_…`), `a_tree_updated_in_place_is_the_tree_built_anew` (both kinds, and a switch between them), `the_tree_folds_each_range_as_one_by_one` (both kinds) and `whole_minor_units_give_the_exact_range_minimum` (new).
- **Mutants:** no batch this turn; the rows of `check_booked` still hold. For T, the new kernel functions: `lows.rs` `Fig::{zero, plus, below, is_negative, quantity}`, `figures`, `in_minor_units`, `RangeMinimum::{of, lowest_change, in_minor_units}` and `Sums::updated`'s switch of kind; `plan.rs` `booked_legs`. ## 2026-11-10 M1, G10: decisions over movements, `next`, an unordered cycle, a prefix walk, the planted fault; mutation batch MB4 (issue #108)

The builder of the M1 run, group G10 of `tmp/m1/build-plan.md` (§3 G10, §4 MB4, §5 the hook), on 834d9f5 (T's C8), picks P8, P13, P19, P25, P55, P57 and P59 read. Each red id was reproduced first on the debug binary; edges were probed on refcore and the engine outside the tests (`tmp/m1/g10/`), each byte-equal unless said.

- **P59 first (778ee6a):** `tally_cli` re-exports the library (`pub use tally;`), for T's `m1_lows` fence test.
- **Bindings and aggregates over a kind's movements (`book/members.rs`).** A binding `over` a span takes the decisions' runs from the walk (`decided_members`: the walk the read is in, up to its point, else the main walk; a run the walk hasn't reached is not a member), a plan's instances as before; a member carries its day and id, so `s.day`, `o.cash.amount` read it. `instance_by_id` finds a decision's run the same way, `RUN-ORDER decision <id> is read before it has run` where the read comes first. A decision whose binding reaches past its own day over a kind the walk books is `RUN-FUTURE a decision on <t> binds over <K> through <d'>, after its day` (`binds_future`). A member with no observation is `unfulfilled:` when dated before the cut (`unfulfilled`). Pads (the walk's own movements) stay not built.
- **`next(K, d)` (E §4.3, `next_movement`):** the first movement of K on or after d, `RUN-TIE` for two on that day, a hole day spread, `HOLE-NOTYET m: no K yet, and its span reaches past the cut` when none.
- **An unordered cycle (`walk/order.rs`, `run_cycle` and its helpers):** decisions of one `order_groups` component due on one day are judged together: each member's reads at its point (`cycle_reads`, the places its legs touch and the moves, kinds, things it reads), its `when` before the others run (`when_before`); a written order or a `ties by` ranks them (`cycle_ranks`, `cycle_after`), else `RUN-ORDER decisions <a> and <b> read each other on <d>` or `RUN-TIE` (`mutual`); a refused member is recorded as a refused run at its places (`refused_run`). N3: a refused point no longer swallows `LOAD-*` or `ENGINE-INVARIANT` (`refused_at_point`, `run_time`, R-66).
- **A leg's read whose walk needs a cell still being computed (F4g, F4h; refcore's `guarded_walk`, `prefix_walk`, `plan_list`).** The attempt is given up (the named stop `GUARDED`, `eval/context.rs`, beside `UNSET_AXIS`; nothing past the hit computed or kept; first a `Stop` variant, which broke the protected fuzz targets' exhaustive matches, so check full's fuzz step, and was folded into a named `NotBuilt` before the last commit), the cut marked blocked while those cells are open, and the read takes a walk up to the leg's day (`walk/reads.rs` `prefix_walk`, kept per (cut, day), a walk to a later day serving an earlier one), whose plan pass lists a plan's instances dated up to that day only (`walk/plan.rs` `plan_list`, `Seed.upto`); a decision, a move whose legs state their own days, or one whose list is already known is taken whole. Such a walk keeps no warnings and no signature (`close_walk`).
- **A decision whose day is a hole (`walk/agenda.rs` `hole_days`, `hole_places`, `Act::PlaceHole`; refcore's `dec_hole`):** the places its legs name, read at the cut, are holes in every unit from the walk's start, the others standing; every place where they can't be read. Read only for an instance that reaches it.
- **A written order with a plan:** load refuses `before` a plan and drops `after` one (L §5.14.2), as refcore does, so the walk's check (`not_yet`) could never fire: gone.
- **The planted fault (P8, INTERFACE §4):** `#[cfg(feature = "fault-injection")] pub enum Fault { Conservation }` (`walk/day.rs`, re-exported as `tally::Fault`); `tally::run_faulted(given: Vec<Given>, request: &Request, fault: Fault) -> Result<Answer, NotImplemented>` and `tally_cli::run_faulted(args: &[OsString], files: &[u8], fault: tally::Fault) -> Output` (a command other than `run` answered as `run` answers it). On the first day a walk that is no projection's books a leg whole, the day's close adds 0.01 to its first unit's conservation sum before invariant 1 (`plant_fault`), once per run across asks and worlds (`ask::How` carries memo, lows and fault; the plant goes back to the world after each ask). It prints `refused: ENGINE-INVARIANT conservation: the legs booked on <day> in <unit> sum to <x>, not 0 (E §9 invariant 1)` at each value on the broken walk, exit 0. On `codes/m1_invariant.refcore`'s `decide/conservation` it gives the stored bytes (checked once by a scratch test, not committed: R-93); the builder's `tally-cli/tests/fault.rs` holds its own reality to refcore's bytes with and without the plant. R1: T's `m1_invariant` target can be wired now.
- **The follow-ups:** F4 / G1 N11 (a movement as a def's key, `RUN-KEY`): closed; G1's probes `w1`–`w3` (refcore 0) and `r1`–`r3` (36, 36, 12) are byte-equal, so T can release them. G6 fix NB8 `lifespan(k)` (`eval/builtin.rs` `lifespan_of`): built, an open, an ended and a hole-ended person byte-equal. G8 review, `gap` read in a decision's walk: it now reads the running walk as it stands, as refcore's `main_walk` does (`walk/reads.rs` `gap`), byte-equal. G2 walk note `refused_legs` by `at_cut`: a refused re-measure reads its places at its own cut (bd55eef); refcore reads min(t, K), and no output reads those places yet (the adjustment refuses the original leg's places), so no vector. G2 walk note `left out:` for a refused decision outside [S, E]: already noted (`schedule_decision` notes it before any refusal check; refcore drops it, #104). G2 walk note `Day::END`: a plan whose day reads a hole prints that hole (carried 7's `w_day_hole`, green); `Day::END` stays only on the legs of an instance refused before its day, which has no hole and no day, and which no reader dates. G6 fix NB1 (a decision before `from` skips the whole check): = refcore, left. G3 NB3 (`first_in_order` with `Brings::ToReader`): unreachable, since `first`/`last` over a movement binding needs a `ties by` (LOAD-UNIT otherwise) and so runs `first_by_ties`.
- **Not built, for later:** `ties by` over values of two kinds (`order.rs` `value_cmp`): the spec's "the one with the least e" has no meaning across kinds, refcore sorts by its internal key (tag, then the rational before the unit, so 5 USD before 10 PLN), and load's types keep one kind per term, so no input was found that reaches it: a question for the round, no STOP-REPORT. `first`/`last` over two bindings with a `ties by` returns a tuple (`(2026-01, big_gift[2026-01])` on refcore), and the engine has no tuple value (`eval/extremes.rs` `bare_key`): G14 or M2. `for p in persons` where a person's lifespan ends at a stated hole (`eval/bindings.rs`). A leg's read whose prefix walk needs a hole day.

### Addendum 2026-11-10: G10 review's B1 and B2 (`tmp/m1/review-g10.md`)

- **B1, `lifespan(k)` records its read.** `eval/builtin.rs` `lifespan_of` now calls `note_thing` on k (its scenario sections in the read's world), as the membership test and a binding over a kind do, and reads the span at the read's own cut and world, not the timeline's. A movement before a scenario's `from` that reads a lifespan the scenario changes is now listed (E §9 item 3, L §6.4), where it was `ENGINE-INVARIANT`. `p/f/min.tally` + `min_s.tally`: `b1: 10.00 PLN`, `before from: pay on 2026-02-01 reads ola`, `unfulfilled: pay`. The same listing shows on `life` + `life_s` (with `pay2 on 2026-02-01 removed by ends`), `law` + `law_s`, the agent's `life/base` + `scen` (`200.00 PLN`, `reads olek`), and the decision form `dec` + `min_s` (exit 69 before; refcore gives `10.00 PLN` with no listing, P46). Refcore still refuses `ENGINE-INVARIANT` on the plan forms (#121).
- **B2, a `never` law date refuses only its value.** In `walk/tuples.rs`, `move_law` refuses `at lifespan(p).to` for a living p as `RUN-NONE never in the law date of <move>`, as refcore's `date_of` does (L §3.6, §5.7). The other asks are answered. Before, `walk/plan.rs` `date_of` refused the whole run `LOAD-UNIT`. `p/f/law.tally`: refcore's bytes (`b0: refused: RUN-NONE never in the law date of pay`, `l0: never`, exit 0), where it was exit 1. `law2.tally` (a closed lifespan) is unchanged and still the same as refcore's.
- **NB1, left (for G14).** Letting `LOAD-*` and `ENGINE-INVARIANT` pass in `prefix_walk`'s arm and in `leg_refused`'s main-walk arm turns `m1_checks_g10` red. On `I:X:t@min_F4g` and `…F4h` the engine gives `ENGINE-INVARIANT a booked plan amount changed: pay[2027-02] booked at 2027-01-01 differs between the cuts 2027-01-01 and 2027-01-01 (E §9 invariant 5)`, where refcore gives `0.00 PLN`. So the old arms hide a false invariant 5. `pay[m]`'s amount `p[m]` reads `ovs` over m, and the guarded read evaluates `pay[2027-02]` again at the same booking cut with other legs. `check_booked` keys by (instance, booking cut); refcore's `inv5` keys by (instance, cut, timeline). Reverted. G14's order: invariant 5's key, or the guarded walk's instances, then NB1, with F4g/F4h as the vector.
- **NB8, left (for G14); NB5 with it.** It isn't small. The refusal is wider than decisions: any unkeyed move's leg read by name is refused at load, a plan's as well as a decision's. `move yy: pays` with `amount floor`, no `reads`, and `ask a_y: yy.cash.amount` gives `LOAD-FIELD cash is not a field of yy`, where refcore gives `95.00 PLN` (`tmp/m1/g10fix/nb8a.tally` to `nb8c.tally`). The text is `load/keyed.rs` `movement_field`'s with the move's id as the kind, but `move_value` gives the kind `pays`. So the id comes in by another path, which I didn't find within the turn.

## 2026-11-10 M1, G11: `explain` of a def cell, an `as if` and an extreme; `inputs-of`; `readers-of` (issue #108)

The builder of the M1 run, group G11 of `tmp/m1/build-plan.md`, on 00b5df9. Each red id was reproduced first on the debug binary; edges were probed on refcore and the engine outside the tests (`tmp/m1/g11/`), byte-equal unless said.

- **`explain` (E §10, L §5.18).** The line's cone is evaluated again with tracing on, in a memo of the trace's own, the ask's set aside and back after it (refcore's explain arm). `eval/trace.rs` (new) records the nodes; `ask/explain.rs` (new) prints them in refcore's order, a worklist, `[n]` where a node first prints and `(<label>: see [n])` after, a row by its stable id, a def cell by its node.
  - **A def cell:** its rules by label, each with its state: `won (version v, source s)` from the rule's `v`, else its version; `beaten by a, b`; `not applicable`; `unknown`; `candidate`. They show once the candidates are known, so a conflict or a refused `when` shows none, as refcore. Under a rule, what its `when` read; under the winner, what its value read; what else the cell read beside them; a `given` row with its `given` line.
  - **A row:** `version, source, "title", locator, governs <span>` or `at <day> [start|end]`, `known <day>`; a band row `<figure>: row <interval> (version v[, "locator"])`.
  - **`min`, `max`, `argmin`, `argmax`,** the aggregates with a term and the built-ins: what the terms traced is dropped, and the first term in binding order whose value (an `argmin`'s key) is the result is read again alone (refcore's `trace_winner`); a hole or a window wins nothing.
  - **An `as if`:** `as if {...}`, then `the pins, evaluated outside the as if` with what the pins read and one `pin <text>` each (value pins by item, then `law`, `known`, `wording … for`, axis options, `scenario`; refcore's `pin_lines`), `inside the as if: v`, `outside the as if: v`. The outside read brings nothing to the register; its refusal prints in its place, a load refusal refuses. A read inside that stops leaves the side's label bare and nothing outside, as refcore.
  - **An ask's `with` pins** (refcore's `explain_pins`): `the pins, evaluated outside them`, `inside the pins: v`, `outside the pins: v`, the outside traced again in the line's context before its pins, at the line's axis options less those the pins fix (`Engine.unpinned`, `outside_pins`).
  - **The work stack:** a cell computed from it while tracing hangs apart, then the read that replays references it, its children started again (`Mark.trace`).
  - **The frames.** The first `check fast` with tracing overflowed the stack of `s_kernel_g7_reads`: the runner calls the CLI in-process on a 2 MiB test thread, where the CLI's main thread has 8 MiB and passed `kernel/g7/deep*.tally`. The trace's helpers now return nothing and keep their first stop for the trace's end, and a def cell's rule states are written on its node as they're found. So `def_cell`'s and `rules`' frames hold a node's place and no vector, string or result of the trace. Green after, and the deep fixtures still byte-equal.
- **`inputs-of`** (E §10, §3.2): the rows a read met are kept in `Collected.rows` (stable id, version, locator) by an engine answering `inputs-of` only (`Rows::Kept`, so a bulk run keeps none). A walk read stands for its rows: each reading's row (`book/reading.rs` `readings`) and each observed row (`book/observe.rs` `seen_observations`) is noted in its collector, which the walk's entries carry to the reader. The head prints bare, then `  <sid> (source s "title"[, locator "l"])` by stable id. Over axes, lines group by row set (`Printed::same`).
- **`readers-of`** (E §10, L §5.18): static, the item graph's inverse, breadth first, each layer by id; tuples read first only for a hole domain; an index, a non-event kind, a unit, a version, a source or a cut: `nothing reads x`; an event kind: not built (STOP-REPORT below).
- **A bare key under `explain` or `inputs-of`** (666cfdc, found by the first check full): `ask x: explain y` with no y gave `x: y`, where refcore refuses `LOAD-KEY ambiguous key y` at the ask's line, as the engine does for a values line (P39). Load now keeps the ask's place when the reference types as an unresolved key literal (`load/check.rs` `ask_body`), and both lines check it after the read. The builder's tests `parse_cases` `a_construct_built_later_is_not_implemented` and `tally-cli` `a_directory_stands_for_its_tally_files`, which took `explain y` as the construct built later, now take `pow(r, r) to 0.01`, a transcendental value (M6); `the_forms_of_milestone_m0_parse`'s `explain x` is now that refusal.
- **Not built, for G12:** `explain` of a movement instance, a balance, a projection or `low`, a walk-bound def, and of any line whose causes reach the walk or a structure it shares (`Trace.walked`, noted at `main_walk` and `reads_apart`; STOP-REPORT below); a reading `after` a movement among the causes. **For later:** `explain` under an axis pin, whose outside read has no option for the axis (refcore prints its own `REFCORE-NOT-YET` there; the engine stops on `UNSET_AXIS`); the fold and `readers-of` an event kind (STOP-REPORTs below). Seen on the way, not G11's: `pow(r, r)` with no `to u` is `LOAD-TO pow needs to u` at load on refcore, `not implemented` at run on the engine (`tmp/m1/g11/un/w.tally`).
- **Probes** (`tmp/m1/g11/`): `v/` (the five vectors' texts), `ai/` (14 asks: the extremes, `as if` over pins, an expression pin, a law pin, nested, inside a def read twice, `with` pins), `ai2/` (13: a 40-step recurrence past the counted depth, alone and under an `as if`, `as if {scenario s}` and `{law …}`, an axis fan-out with and without pins, `with known`, `with scenario`, `ties by`, hole terms, `explain` per binding tuple), `st/` (6: a conflict, unknown `when`s, no rule, a refused read inside an `as if`), `ro/` (16 `readers-of`: inputs, defs, a band, a place, a move, an axis, an index, kinds, persons, a version, a source, an ask; then `explain` and `inputs-of` of defs reading a band and a balance, 5), `g7/` (the deep fixtures). All byte-equal except `ai2` `e_pick_a` (refcore's not-yet), `ro` `e_b` and `r_fees` (the STOP-REPORTs).
- **Ids:** `U:ask/ASK-05`, `U:val/VAL-03`, `U:scen/SCEN-03/SCEN-03`, `U:set/scen/SCEN-03/SCEN-03`, `later/inputs_of_balance` (`m1_checks_g11`): green. `milestone-report: M1: 64/72` → `65/72`; the 7 red are G12–G14's.
- **Files** (under `crates/tally/src/`), for the map (P19). New: `eval/trace.rs` (the trace: nodes, rule states, the winner, the work stack's replays, the walk noted); `ask/explain.rs` (the three asks' lines and the derivation's print). Changed: `eval/rules.rs` (a def cell traced, its rules' states), `eval/context.rs` (the traced `as if`, `pin_lines`, `outside_pins`), `eval/read.rs` (a row read traced and noted; `row_value` split out), `eval/band.rs` (a band row), `eval/aggregate.rs` and `eval/builtin.rs` (the extremes' winner), `eval/stack.rs` (a replay's trace), `eval/memo.rs` (`Cells::emptied`, `reads_apart` notes the walk), `eval/state.rs` (`Collected.rows`, `RowSeen`, `Rows`, `Engine.trace`, `keep_rows`, `unpinned`), `ask/answer.rs` (the routes, the outside context), `ask/check.rs` (`Printed.body`, `bare`, `lines`, `same`), `ask/axes.rs` (a line's body under its head), `lib.rs`, `load/check.rs` (the bare key), `tests/parse_cases.rs` and `tally-cli/src/lib.rs` (the builder's tests' construct built later); kernel: `book/reading.rs`, `book/observe.rs`, `book/ledger.rs`, `walk/agenda.rs`, `walk/reads.rs`.
- **Size:** 23 files, +1 746 −91 in engine/crates from 00b5df9 (planned +450 −50, expected +720 −80): about 2.4× the expected. `trace.rs` is 673 lines and `explain.rs` 456 (147 and 79 of them comments); the `with` pins form, the walk's note and the frames' rework weren't in the plan. Kernel +34 −14.
- **Kernel functions touched:** `book/reading.rs` `readings` (its `Rows`, a reading's row noted), `first_readings` (the call); `book/observe.rs` `seen_observations` (the same), `observations` (the call); `book/ledger.rs` (two calls of `readings`); `walk/agenda.rs` `plan_walk` (the call); `walk/reads.rs` `main_walk` (`trace_walk`).
- **Mutants** (`mutants-local --next -F '<the kernel functions above>'` over `book/reading.rs`, `book/observe.rs`, `walk/reads.rs`, at 666cfdc, niced; the regex in `tmp/m1/g11/mb-regex.txt`; `plan_walk` left out, its one changed argument being `readings`'): 25 mutants in 16 min, 17 caught, 3 missed, 5 unviable. **Missed, for T:**
  - `seen_observations`' `rows == Rows::Kept` → `!=` (`observe.rs:584`, G11's): no `inputs-of` reads an observed place, so nothing tells the observed rows listed from not. Needed: `inputs-of` of a def reading `bal` or `gap` of a place with observed rows, beside a twin with none.
